+const isReportToday = (report) => {
+  const dObj = parseDateFromReport(report.thoi_gian);
+  if (!dObj) return false;
+  const now = new Date();
+  return dObj.getDate() === now.getDate() &&
+         dObj.getMonth() === now.getMonth() &&
+         dObj.getFullYear() === now.getFullYear();
+};

+const formatPendingDateDisplay = (timeStr) => {
+  const dObj = parseDateFromReport(timeStr);
+  if (!dObj) return formatDisplayTime(timeStr).date;
+  
+  const now = new Date();
+  const today = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate());
+  const target = new Date(dObj.getFullYear(), dObj.getMonth(), dObj.getDate());
+  
+  const diffTime = target - today;
+  const diffDays = Math.round(diffTime / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
+  
+  if (diffDays === 0) return "H├â┬┤m nay";
+  if (diffDays === -1) return "H├â┬┤m qua";
+  
+  return formatDisplayTime(timeStr).date;
+};

+const moveReportToToday = async (report) => {
+  if (!report) return;
+  if (API_URL === 'YOUR_GOOGLE_APPS_SCRIPT_WEB_APP_URL') {
+    alert("T├â┬¡nh n├ä╞Æng n├â┬áy c├í┬║┬ºn c├í┬║┬Ñu h├â┬¼nh API_URL");
+    return;
+  }
+  const originalTime = report.thoi_gian;
+  
+  const now = new Date();
+  const day = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
+  const month = String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
+  const year = now.getFullYear();
+  let thuNum = now.getDay();
+  const thu = thuNum === 0 ? '8' : String(thuNum + 1);
+  
+  const timeObj = parseTimeString(report.thoi_gian) || { hour: '08', minute: '00' };
+  
+  const newTime = `${timeObj.hour}:${timeObj.minute} /${thu} /${day}/${month}/${year}`;
+  
+  if (report.thoi_gian !== newTime) {
+    report.thoi_gian = newTime;
+    droppedReportId.value = report.id;
+    setTimeout(() => {
+      if (droppedReportId.value === report.id) droppedReportId.value = null;
+    }, 800);
+    
+    try {
+      const response = await fetch(API_URL, {
+        method: 'POST',
+        body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'update', ...report })
+      });
+      const result = await response.json();
+      if (result.status !== 'success') {
+        report.thoi_gian = originalTime;
+        alert('L├í┬╗ΓÇöi c├í┬║┬¡p nh├í┬║┬¡t: ' + result.message);
+      } else {
+        alert('Th├â┬ánh c├â┬┤ng chuy├í┬╗╞Æn th├í┬║┬╗ c├â┬┤ng vi├í┬╗ΓÇíc sang H├â┬┤m nay');

+      report.thoi_gian = originalTime;
+    }
+  }
+};
